# Godsworn: Content Catalogue

A review list of all game content. It is updated each phase as content lands.

## Races (11)

| Race | Stat bonus | Passive | Active skill |
|---|---|---|---|
| Human | +1 all | **Adaptable**: +1 skill point every 10 levels, +5% XP | **Rally**: party +15% damage for 3 turns (6 cd) |
| Elf | INT +3, DEX +2, VIT −2 | **Starlit Blood**: +10 magic resist, +5% arcane damage | **Moonlit Step**: +25% dodge for 2 turns (5 cd) |
| Dwarf | VIT +3, STR +1, DEX −1 | **Stoneborn**: +5% defense, 15% crafting discount | **Stoneform**: take 40% less damage for 2 turns (6 cd) |
| Orc | STR +3, VIT +1, INT −2 | **Bloodfury**: +20% resource gain, +5% physical damage | **Bloodlust**: heal 15% HP, +20% physical damage for 3 turns (6 cd) |
| Beastkin | DEX +3, LUK +1, WIS −1 | **Feral Reflexes**: +3 speed, +5% dodge | **Pounce**: 130% ATK to any row, Bleed (4 cd) |
| Nephilim | FAI +3, WIS +1, VIT −1 | **Celestial Heritage**: +15% favor gain, +10% holy damage, −15% shadow resist | **Radiant Wings**: 90% DIV holy damage to all enemies (6 cd) |

**New Game+ races** (one per two NG+ tiers; unlocked for new heroes once any of your heroes reaches the tier, on any save; no penalties, and each is stronger than the last):

| Race | Unlock | Stat bonus | Passive | Active skill |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| Gnome | NG+2 | INT +3, DEX +2, LUK +3 | **Tinker's Luck**: +15% Magic Find and gold find, +3% crit, +5% XP, 25% crafting discount | **Overclock**: +25% damage, +10 speed for 3 turns (5 cd) |
| Fae | NG+4 | DEX +3, INT +3, WIS +3, LUK +2 | **Glamour**: +8% dodge, +15 magic resist, +10% arcane damage, +10% max MP | **Fey Veil**: party +20% dodge, 15% less damage for 3 turns (6 cd) |
| Giant | NG+6 | STR +5, VIT +5, FAI +2, WIS +2 | **Titan's Frame**: +20% max HP, +10% defense, +12% physical damage | **Earthshaker**: 150% ATK to all enemies, Slow (5 cd) |
| Dragon | NG+8 | STR +4, INT +4, VIT +4, FAI +3, DEX +2 | **Wyrm's Blood**: +15% max HP and defense, +20% fire damage, +40% fire resist, +15% crit damage | **Dragon's Breath**: 220% MAG fire damage to all enemies, Burn (5 cd) |
| Primordial | NG+10 | STR +4, INT +4, VIT +5, DEX/WIS/LUK/FAI +3 | **Before the Gods**: +25% max HP, +20% favor, +15% XP and damage, +10% all resists, 10% less damage taken, +1 skill point every 5 levels | **Primordial Unmaking**: 300% MAG void damage to all enemies, Corruption (5 cd) |

## Classes (8)

Every class starts with 34 primary points and gains 5.0 per level.

| Class | Role | Resource | Mechanic | Base STR/DEX/INT/VIT/WIS/LUK/FAI | Weapons | Armor | Starting skills |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| Warrior | Melee damage / tank | Rage | Rage from dealing/taking damage | 9/5/2/8/3/4/3 | sword, greatsword, axe, greataxe, mace, warhammer, spear, shield | all | Heavy Strike, Rampage |
| Mage | Ranged magic | MP only | Elemental combos (Burn+Ice = Shatter, Wet+Lightning = Conduct) | 2/4/10/4/7/4/3 | staff, wand, tome, orb, dagger | cloth | Firebolt, Frost Shard |
| Rogue | Melee burst | Combo Points | Builders/finishers; stealth guarantees a crit | 5/10/3/5/3/7/1 | dagger, rapier, sword, throwing, crossbow, fist | cloth, leather | Backstab, Eviscerate |
| Cleric | Healer / holy | Faith | Overhealing becomes a shield | 4/3/4/6/6/3/8 | mace, warhammer, staff, tome, shield | cloth, leather, mail | Smite, Mend |
| Ranger | Ranged physical / pet | Focus | Pet companion; Focus stacks add ranged damage | 4/9/3/6/5/5/2 | bow, crossbow, spear, axe, dagger, throwing | leather, mail | Aimed Shot, Sic 'Em |
| Necromancer | Summoner / shadow | Soul Shards | Corpses power summons | 2/3/9/5/8/3/4 | scythe, staff, wand, dagger, orb, tome | cloth | Soul Bolt, Raise Skeleton |
| Monk | Melee hybrid | Chi | Tiger / Crane / Serpent stances | 6/8/2/6/5/4/3 | fist, staff, spear | cloth, leather | Palm Strike, Tiger Stance |
| Bard | Support / arcane | Verse | Song auras; switching songs triggers a Crescendo | 3/6/6/5/5/6/3 | instrument, rapier, sword, dagger, wand | cloth, leather | Discordant Note, Song of Valor |

## Class resources (7)
Rage (100, decays 5/turn), Combo Points (5), Faith (100, starts 20), Focus (5), Soul Shards (5, starts 1), Chi (5, starts 1), Verse (100).

## Gods (10 + 1 hidden)
Solenne (Sun & Justice, holy), Morrath (Death & Endings, shadow), Kael (War & Glory, physical), Ysolde (Nature & the Hunt, nature), Vex (Trickery & Luck, arcane), Thalor (Forge & Craft, fire), Nymira (Seas & Storms, lightning), Aurelis (Knowledge & Magic, arcane), Hesha (Hearth & Mercy, holy), Grimvald (Wealth & Trade, physical). Hidden: **The Nameless One** (Corruption & the Void, void). Favor rules, blessings and divine items are in *Gods, favor and blessings* below.

## Equipment types
**Weapons (20):** Sword, Greatsword, Axe, Greataxe, Mace, Warhammer, Dagger, Rapier, Spear, Scythe, Fist Weapon, Bow, Crossbow, Throwing Weapon, Staff, Wand, Tome, Orb, Instrument, Shield.

**Armor weights (4):** Cloth, Leather, Mail, Plate.

## Paragon board (19 nodes)
Might/Finesse/Intellect/Fortitude/Insight/Fortune/Devotion (+1 to one primary per rank, max 50) · Vigor (+1% HP, 30) · Precision (+0.2% crit, 25) · Swiftness (+1 speed, 10) · Prosperity (+1% gold find, 50) · Treasure Sense (+0.5% magic find, 50) · Warding (+0.5% all resists, 40). Difficulty-gated: Grit and Ferocity (Hard+), Legend Lore and Killing Edge (Legendary+), Defiance and Godslayer (Impossible only).

## Progression
- XP to next level: `floor(100 × level^1.55)` (level 1 → 100, level 10 → 3,548).
- Kill XP: ×(1 + 0.1 × level difference), clamped to 5–150%.
- Per character level: +3 attribute points and a full restore. Per class level: +1 skill point, +1 more every 5 levels.
- Level cap: 60 (80 in NG+). Paragon levels after the cap.
- Respec: 50 gold × level. Starting gold: 50.

## Stats

**Primary (7):** STR, DEX, INT, VIT, WIS, LUK, FAI

**Gear inputs (5):** Weapon Damage, Weapon Magic, Relic Power, Armor, Armor Resist

**Derived (28):**

| Stat | Formula / base | Cap |
|---|---|---|
| Max HP | 50 + VIT×10 + level×5 | min 1 |
| Max MP | 20 + INT×4 + WIS×3 + level×2 | |
| Physical Attack | STR×2 + weapon damage | |
| Magic Attack | INT×2 + weapon magic | |
| Divine Power | FAI×2 + relic power | |
| Defense | VIT + armor | |
| Magic Resist | WIS + armor resist | |
| Crit Chance | 5% + LUK×0.5% | 60% |
| Crit Damage | 150% | min 100% |
| Dodge | DEX×0.4% | 35% |
| Accuracy | 90% + DEX×0.2% | 100% |
| Speed | 10 + DEX×0.5 | min 1 |
| Lifesteal | 0% | 30% |
| Cooldown Reduction | 0% | 40% |
| Status Chance | LUK×0.2% | |
| Status Resist | WIS×0.3% + VIT×0.1% | 75% |
| Gold Find | LUK×1% | |
| Magic Find | LUK×0.5% | |
| Favor Gain | FAI×1% | |
| Blessing Power | FAI×1% | |
| XP Gain | 0% | |
| Healing Power | WIS×1% + FAI×1% | |
| Healing Received | 0% | |
| Damage Bonus | 0% | |
| Damage Taken | 100% | min 10% |
| Resource Gain | 0% | |
| HP Regen | 0 / turn | |
| MP Regen | WIS×0.1 / turn | |

**Elemental (9 elements × damage + resist):** Physical, Fire, Ice, Lightning, Nature, Holy, Shadow, Arcane, Void. Resist range is −100% to 200%, and anything above 100% absorbs.

## Engine vocabulary
- **Trigger hooks (14):** onHit, onCrit, onKill, onDamageTaken, onDodge, onTurnStart, onTurnEnd, onLowHp, onSkillCast, onHeal, onStatusApplied, onCombatStart, onCombatEnd, onDeath
- **Effect kinds (15):** damage, heal, restoreMp, gainResource, applyStatus, removeStatus, shield, buff, favor, reduceCooldowns, castSkill, gold, setStance, startSong, summon
- **Modifier types (4):** flat, percent (additive), more (multiplicative), override. Any modifier can carry a condition, and derived/elemental modifiers can scale `per` a primary or gear stat (e.g. +1 Physical Attack per DEX).
- **Condition kinds (20):** always, hpBelow, hpAbove, inCombat, timeOfDay, weather, weaponType, skillTag, element, hasStatus, targetHasStatus, targetType, targetHpBelow, patron, stance, resourceAtLeast, flag, all, any, not
- **Skill tags (31):** Attack, Spell, Melee, Ranged, Projectile, AoE, DoT, Heal, Buff, Debuff, Summon, Pet, Song, Stance, Movement, Finisher, Builder, Curse, Shout, Physical, Fire, Ice, Lightning, Nature, Poison, Holy, Shadow, Arcane, Void, Divine, Racial

### Consumables (133)

- Minor Health Potion: Restores 25% of max HP.
- Minor Mana Potion: Restores 40 MP.
- Minor Spirit Tonic: Restores 20% of your class resource.
- Lesser Health Potion: Restores 35% of max HP.
- Lesser Mana Potion: Restores 100 MP.

- Greater Health Potion: Restores 50% of max HP.

- Major Health Potion: Restores 70% of max HP.
- Major Mana Potion: Restores 450 MP.
- Major Spirit Tonic: Restores 65% of your class resource.
- Supreme Health Potion: Restores 100% of max HP.
- Supreme Mana Potion: Restores 900 MP.
- Supreme Spirit Tonic: Restores 100% of your class resource.

**Mechanic flags (18):** `firstStrike` (Always act first in the opening round.) · `reviveOnce` (Once per battle, survive a killing blow with 1 HP.) · `burnSpreadsOnDeath` (Burning enemies spread Burn to their allies when they die.) · `bonusVsBosses` (Deal 25% more damage to bosses.) · `strongerAtNight` (Deal 20% more damage at night.) · `strongerByDay` (Deal 20% more damage during the day.) · `dotsCanCrit` (Damage over time can critically strike.) · `critsIgnoreDefense` (Critical hits ignore half of the target’s defense and resistance.) · `reach` (Melee attacks can strike the back row.) · `overhealShield` (Half of all overhealing becomes a shield.) · `stealthOnCombatStart` (Begin every battle in stealth.) · `shatterAlways` (Ice damage against Burning enemies always Shatters.) · `twinSongs` (Two songs can play at once.) · `extraSummon` (Summoning skills raise one extra minion.) · `executioner` (Attacks against enemies below 20% HP always crit.) · `spellEcho` (20% chance for spells to cast a second time for free.) · `bloodMagic` (Skills cost HP instead of MP.) · `armorTraining` (No speed penalty for wearing armor only a secondary class allows.) · `immune:<status>`

### Status effects (47)

| Status | Kind | Effect |
|---|---|---|
| **Burn** | debuff | Takes fire damage each turn. Ice damage on a Burning target can Shatter. |
| **Poison** | debuff | Takes nature damage each turn and receives 5% less healing per stack. |
| **Bleed** | debuff | Takes physical damage each turn. |
| **Freeze** | debuff | Frozen solid: loses its turn and has 25% less defense. |
| **Stun** | debuff | Loses its next turn. |
| **Slow** | debuff | 30% less speed. |
| **Blind** | debuff | -40% accuracy. |
| **Silence** | debuff | Cannot use skills. |
| **Weaken** | debuff | 25% less physical and magic attack. |
| **Vulnerable** | debuff | Takes 25% more damage. |
| **Charm** | debuff | Beguiled: attacks its own allies. |
| **Fear** | debuff | 50% chance to cower and lose the turn; 15% less damage. |
| **Curse** | debuff | Receives 50% less healing and resists statuses less. |
| **Corruption** | debuff | Void eats away at body and soul: void damage each turn and -8% max HP per stack. |
| **Wet** | debuff | Soaked: -25% lightning resist, +25% fire resist. Lightning on a Wet target can Conduct. |
| **Regen** | buff | Heals 6% of max HP each turn. |
| **Haste** | buff | 30% more speed. |
| **Berserk** | buff | +30% damage and +10% crit, but takes 15% more damage. |
| **Divine Protection** | buff | Takes 50% less damage. |
| **Taunt** | buff | Goading the enemy: their single-target attacks must hit you. |
| **Winding Up** | buff | Winding up a devastating attack for its next turn. Break its stagger bar to interrupt it, or Defend. |
| **Enraged** | buff | The fight has gone on too long: +40% damage and 10% more speed per stack, growing over time. |
| **Stealth** | buff | Hidden: the next attack is a guaranteed critical hit. Enemies cannot single you out. |
| **Sleep** | debuff | Fast asleep: loses its turns until it takes damage. |
| **Confuse** | debuff | Befuddled: 35% chance to lose the turn and 40 less accuracy. |
| **Petrify** | debuff | Turned to stone: loses its turns, but takes 40% less damage and has double defense. |
| **Doom** | debuff | Marked for the Last Door: loses 8% of max HP each turn and cannot be healed well (-50%). |
| **Entangle** | debuff | Held by roots or wire: 25% chance to lose the turn, 20 less dodge and 20% less speed. |
| **Mired** | debuff | Stuck in mud or sand: 25% less speed and 15 less dodge. |
| **Marked** | debuff | A target painted on its back: takes 10% more damage and has 15 less dodge. |
| **Inspired** | buff | +10% damage and +8% crit chance. |
| **Earthbound** | buff | +25% defense and +30% earth resist, 5% less speed. |
| **Fel Taint** | buff | Corruption you carry: +3% damage per stack, but it eats 0.6% of your max HP each turn per stack. |
| **Overheated** | debuff | The machinery screams: take 25% more damage and burn for a little each turn. |
| **Tempo** | buff | The rhythm is building: +4% damage and +2 speed per stack. |
| **Witch-Mark** | debuff | Marked for judgment: takes 12% more damage and its spells are 20% weaker. |
| **Quaking Ground** | debuff | The ground heaves: speed -25%, 2% of max HP lost each turn. |
| **Firm Footing** | buff | You stand on the stable ground: +10% defense. |
| **Blinding Facets** | debuff | Light scatters off the crystal: accuracy -15. |
| **Crystal Ward** | buff | The crystal rings around you: +25% magic resist and 10% less damage taken. |
| **Blowing Sand** | debuff | Sand in the eyes: accuracy -25. |
| **Sand Cover** | buff | The dunes hide you: +10% dodge. |
| **Scorched Ground** | debuff | The floor is lava: 4% of max HP lost to fire each turn. |
| **Heat Shield** | buff | The heat feeds you: +8% damage. |
| **Dream Sleep** | debuff | Asleep: loses its turns. Any damage wakes it. |
| **Nightmare** | debuff | Tormented: loses 3% of max HP each turn and may flinch away (25% to lose its turn). |

## Skill trees

Generated from the data files by `npm run content:md`.

<!-- trees:start -->
85 class trees, 1696 nodes. Each branch has 2 active skills (each with 2 modifier nodes), 4 minor passives, 2 notables, a choice pair, a keystone and a capstone across 6 tiers. Tier t needs (t - 1) x 5 points in the branch.

### Warrior (68 nodes)

| Branch | Skills (modifiers) | Notables | Choice | Keystone | Capstone |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| Arms | **Heavy Strike** (Sundering Blow, Cleaving Strike); **Mortal Strike** (Deep Wounds, Overpower) | Blood in the Water, Bleeding Edge | Two-Handed Mastery / Sword and Board | Reckless Abandon: Keystone: +30% damage, but take 20% more damage. | Colossus Smash |
| Fury | **Rampage** (Unrelenting, Bloodthirst); **Enrage** (Frenzy, Blood Rage) | Battle Trance, Raging Blows | Seething / Controlled Fury | Blood Frenzy: Keystone: +8% lifesteal, but healing you receive is halved. | Avatar of War |
| Protection | **Shield Wall** (Bulwark, Last Bastion); **Shield Slam** (Concussive Slam, Ricochet) | Iron Skin, Second Wind | Stalwart / Thorns | Unbreakable: Keystone: take 20% less damage, but deal 20% less. | Immortal |
| Warcraft | **Battle Shout** (Commanding Shout, Intimidating Shout); **Whirlwind** (Bladestorm, Sweeping Strikes) | Opportunist, Momentum | Veteran / Brute | Glory Hound: Keystone: +15% damage and +25% XP, but -15% defense. | Unstoppable Force |
| Secrets |  | Scar Tissue, Veteran’s Grip, Unyielding, Warlord’s Mark |  | :  |  |

## Not yet added
Elite affixes, the bestiary, achievements and the codex (Phase 13), NG+ content (Phase 14), endgame activities and Primordial items (Phase 15).

## Specializations (24)

<!-- specializations:start -->
72 specializations, 3 per class, chosen at class level 20. Each adds a 12-node branch (signature skill with 2 modifiers, 3 minors, Signature Mastery, 2 notables, a choice pair, a capstone; tiers need (t - 1) x 4 points) and replaces the class ultimate.

| Class | Specialization | Signature skill (modifiers) | Ultimate | Capstone |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| Warrior | **Berserker**: Unbound fury: the closer to death, the deadlier. | Frenzied Assault (Blood Spray, Endless Frenzy) | **Bloodstorm**: A storm of blades: 300% ATK to every enemy, heal 20% of max HP and go Berserk. | Undying Rage |
| Warrior | **Paladin**: A holy warrior: steel and faith as one. | Crusader Strike (Judgement Strike, Blessed Strike) | **Hammer of Heaven**: A hammer of light: 220% ATK holy damage to every enemy and Divine Protection for the party. | Lay on Hands: The first time you fall below 30% HP each battle, heal 50%. |
| Warrior | **Warlord**: A commander who wins battles before they start. | Rallying Banner (War Banner, Iron Banner) | **Grand Offensive**: Lead the charge: 200% ATK to every enemy (30% Stun) and Haste the party. | Supreme Commander: The party starts every battle with +20% damage for 3 turns. |
| Rogue | **Assassin**: One target. One strike. No witnesses. | Assassinate (Silent Kill, Deathblow) | **Thousand Cuts**: Ten flashing cuts on one enemy, 50% ATK each, every one a critical hit. | Angel of Death |
| Rogue | **Shadowdancer**: Never where the blade falls. | Shadow Step (Umbral Blade, Shadow Veil) | **Shadow Storm**: Shadows erupt: 250% ATK shadow damage to every enemy, and you vanish into Stealth. | One with Darkness |
| Rogue | **Swashbuckler**: Style, luck and a dazzling blade. | Flourish (Dazzling Flourish, Flashy) | **Grand Flourish**: A whirlwind performance: six strikes of 80% ATK at random enemies; the party gains +20% crit for 2 turns. | Legend of the Seas |
| Ranger | **Beastmaster**: The pack is the weapon. | Bestial Wrath (Pack Hunt, Feral Roar) | **Stampede**: A stampede of spirit beasts: 220% ATK nature damage to every enemy, and a spirit stag joins you. | King of the Wild: A second wolf joins every battle. |
| Ranger | **Sharpshooter**: Patience, then one perfect shot. | Deadly Aim (Kill Shot, Penetrating Shot) | **Final Arrow**: One perfect arrow: 600% ATK, always a critical hit. | Perfect Shot |
| Ranger | **Warden**: Guardian of the wild places. | Entangling Roots (Thorned Roots, Binding Roots) | **Wrath of Nature**: The forest rises: 250% ATK nature damage to every enemy with heavy Poison, and the party regenerates. | Heart of the Forest |
| Monk | **Grandmaster**: Perfection of the fist. | Seven-Sided Strike (Focused Strikes, Whirling Strikes) | **Fists of Fury**: Sixteen strikes on one enemy, 40% ATK each. | True Grandmaster |
| Monk | **Mystic**: Spirit made flesh: healing and inner light. | Spirit Palm (Radiant Palm, Soothing Palm) | **Nirvana**: Perfect calm: heal 60% of max HP, gain Divine Protection and full Chi, and strike every enemy for 150% ATK. | Enlightened One |
| Monk | **Wind Dancer**: Untouchable as the wind. | Cyclone Kick (Gale Kick, Wind Wall) | **Tempest Dance**: A dance like a storm: ten strikes of 50% ATK at random enemies, and you are Hasted. | Eye of the Hurricane |
| Mage | **Pyromancer**: Fire, and nothing but fire. | Meteor (Meteor Shower, Impact) | **Sunfall**: Drop a piece of the sun: 320% MAG fire to every enemy with 3 stacks of Burn. | Lord of Flame: Fire crits explode on every enemy for 30% MAG. |
| Mage | **Chronomancer**: Master of time: act more, wait less. | Time Warp (Accelerate, Slow Time) | **Time Stop**: Freeze time: Stun every enemy, Haste the party and reduce your cooldowns by 3. | Master of Time: Skills have a 20% chance to reduce your cooldowns by 1. |
| Mage | **Stormcaller**: Ride the storm: soak, then strike. | Storm Surge (Tidal Surge, Static Surge) | **Maelstrom**: A raging maelstrom: every enemy is soaked, then eight bolts of 70% MAG lightning strike at random. | Avatar of Storms |
| Cleric | **Inquisitor**: Hunter of heretics. The light that burns. | Condemn (Purge, Zealous Condemnation) | **Final Judgement**: The gods’ verdict: 300% DIV holy damage to every enemy. | Grand Inquisitor |
| Cleric | **Oracle**: She sees the blow before it falls. | Foresight (Prophecy, Clairvoyance) | **Divine Prophecy**: Fate bends: heal the party for 50% of max HP, then grant Divine Protection and Haste. | Voice of the Gods |
| Cleric | **Saint**: Mercy made flesh. No one dies on her watch. | Sacred Renewal (Radiant Renewal, Blessed Renewal) | **Resurrection**: Life without limit: fully heal the party and grant Regen and Divine Protection. | Living Saint: At the start of your turn, every ally heals 6% of max HP. |
| Necromancer | **Lich**: Undeath, frost and endless spellcraft. | Soul Frost (Frozen Soul, Phylactery Drain) | **Eternal Winter**: The cold of the grave: 260% MAG ice to every enemy, with a 50% chance to Freeze. | Archlich |
| Necromancer | **Bonelord**: An army of bone, and a golem to lead it. | Raise Bone Golem (Reinforced Golem, Bone Wall) | **Bone Apocalypse**: A storm of bone: 240% MAG physical to every enemy, and a Bone Golem rises. | Lord of the Ossuary: Two skeleton warriors rise at the start of every battle. |
| Necromancer | **Plaguebringer**: Sickness that spreads, stacks and never stops. | Plague Bomb (Virulent Bomb, Concentrated) | **Black Death**: The great plague: 200% MAG nature to every enemy, 5 stacks of Poison and a Curse. | Herald of Pestilence |
| Bard | **Virtuoso**: Pure musical genius, sharp enough to cut. | Masterpiece (Encore Performance, Crescendo Strike) | **Symphony of Destruction**: The final movement: 300% MAG arcane damage to every enemy. | Living Legend |
| Bard | **Skald**: War-singer of the north. Songs that make heroes. | War Chant (Chant of Blood, Chant of Iron) | **Ragnarok Chorus**: A chorus to end the world: 250% MAG physical to every enemy, and the whole party goes Berserk. | Voice of Valhalla: Start every battle with the Song of Valor playing and the party Hasted. |
| Bard | **Trickster**: Nothing is what it seems. | Misdirect (Confusion, Mirror Image) | **Grand Illusion**: Nothing is real: every enemy may be Charmed (70%), and the party vanishes into Stealth. | Master of Illusion: Crits may Charm the target. |
| Knight | **Oathbound**: A holy tank: light that heals, a shield that burns. | Consecrated Ground (Radiant Footing, Healing Light) | **Oath Eternal**: The oath answers: 250% DIV holy damage to every enemy, the party is healed for 25% and gains Divine Protection. | Sun Sworn: +10% divine power and heal 5% of max HP at each turn start. |
| Knight | **Dragoon**: A lancer who fights from above and lands like a comet. | Sky Lance (Higher Leap, Wyrmscale) | **Dragonfall**: Fall from the sky: 520% ATK to one enemy, and it is Stunned. | Lord of Lances: +12% physical attack; Sky Lance generates 15 Resolve. |
| Knight | **Bulwark**: The wall that does not move, whatever it costs. | Immovable Wall (Thicker Shell, Spiked) | **Last Bastion**: The whole party is shielded for 35% of max HP and takes 30% less damage for 3 turns. | Bastion Eternal: 15% less damage taken and +10% max HP. |
| Druid | **Wildheart**: Beast-shifter: fury in bear and wolf shapes. | Primal Rampage (Fury of Claws, Gory Frenzy) | **Avatar of the Wild**: You become every beast: 300% ATK to every enemy, heal 20%, and gain +25% damage for 3 turns. | Beastlord’s Will: +10% physical attack and +8% lifesteal in Bear and Wolf forms. |
| Druid | **Moonsage**: Moon-sworn caster: starlight and silver fire. | Starfall (Meteoric, Cold Stars) | **Eclipse**: The sky goes dark: 280% MAG arcane damage to every enemy and they are Blinded; your party gains Haste. | Sage of the Silver Sky: +12% magic attack, 10% cooldown reduction. |
| Druid | **Grovewarden**: The grove’s keeper: healing roots and thorned shields. | Verdant Embrace (Overgrowth, Thorn Ward) | **Worldroot**: The world’s oldest root rises: heal the party for 40%, grant Regeneration, and entangle every enemy for 150% MAG nature damage and Slow. | Eldest of the Grove: +15% healing power and Regeneration is stronger. |
| Warlock | **Demonologist**: Commands demons: imps, infernals and the gate between. | Summon Imp (Twin Imps, Fiery Pact) | **Infernal Gate**: A gate opens: 240% MAG fire damage to every enemy, and a Hollow Infernal steps through to fight for you. | Lord of the Gate: +12% magic attack and summons last twice as long. |
| Warlock | **Hexer**: A curse-spinner: slow, certain, inevitable. | Doom Hex (Deeper Doom, Lingering Doom) | **The Unraveling**: Every enemy’s fate comes undone: 220% MAG shadow damage, Curse, Vulnerable and Weaken on all. | Fate-Spinner: +12% magic attack and 10% cooldown reduction. |
| Warlock | **Voidcaller**: A void-caller: erupt, collapse and call it good. | Void Eruption (Greater Eruption, Hollow Echo) | **Collapse**: The world folds in: 350% MAG void damage to every enemy; you lose all Fel Taint and heal 25%. | Heart of the Void: +15% magic attack and 8% lifesteal on void damage. |
| Summoner | **Elementalist**: Calls the elements themselves: storms, waves and furnaces. | Elemental Storm (Greater Storm, Frostbound) | **Primal Convergence**: Every element at once: 300% MAG to every enemy, and your spirits are healed for 30%. | Four Winds: +15% magic attack. |
| Summoner | **Spiritcaller**: A host of spirits at once: many hands, many voices. | Ancestral Call (Louder Call, Healing Chorus) | **Host of Spirits**: The whole host answers: 250% MAG arcane damage to every enemy, three spirits join you, and you regain 50 Bond. | Warden of Ghosts: +12% magic attack and Bond generation +15%. |
| Summoner | **Golemancer**: Builds with spirit what stone cannot do alone. | Stone Bulwark (Thicker Stone, Quicker Stone) | **Colossus**: A colossus of living stone rises: 260% ATK-equivalent damage to every enemy and it fights for the rest of the battle. | Master Mason: +10% max HP and +10% damage; golems last twice as long. |
| Alchemist | **Grenadier**: Fire everywhere, ideally away from you. | Cluster Bomb (More Bomblets, Sticky Bombs) | **Carpet of Fire**: The whole field burns: 280% MAG fire damage to every enemy and Burn that stacks. | Sapper-General: +15% magic attack; bombs may throw twice. |
| Alchemist | **Mutagenist**: Make yourself a weapon, at some cost. | Chimeric Surge (Longer Surge, Harsher Surge) | **Perfect Form**: The mutation stabilizes: heal 40%, +50% damage and +20% defense for 4 turns, and 200% ATK to every enemy. | The Final Form: +15% physical attack, +12% max HP. |
| Alchemist | **Apothecary**: Heal the whole party before it knows it’s hurt. | Panacea (Stronger Panacea, Long Panacea) | **Elixir of Life**: The final brew: the party is healed for 60%, fallen allies’ pain is gone, and everyone gains Regeneration and Divine Protection. | Physician of the Realm: +20% healing power and healing grants Regeneration. |
| Shaman | **Stormshaman**: The storm walks beside you. | Thunderstrike (Bigger Bolt, Arc Bolt) | **Tempest Called**: The storm answers: 300% MAG lightning damage to every enemy, 30% Stun; your totems pulse again. | Child of Thunder: +15% magic attack and +10% crit chance. |
| Shaman | **Spiritwalker**: Walk between worlds and bring the dead along. | Ancestral Guidance (Fuller Voice, Warding Voice) | **Host of Ancestors**: Your whole line stands behind you: heal the party for 45%, gain Regeneration and +30% damage for 3 turns, and 150% MAG damage to every enemy. | Between Worlds: +20% healing power and 10% less damage taken. |
| Shaman | **Earthwarden**: A wall of stone with a voice. | Earthen Shield (Thicker Shield, Quick Shield) | **Mountain’s Wrath**: The mountain wakes: 260% MAG physical damage to every enemy, Stun, and the party takes 30% less damage for 3 turns. | Warden of the Mountain: +15% max HP and 12% less damage taken. |
| Chronomancer | **Timeweaver**: Direct the order of the battle, one thread at a time. | Chrono Loop (Looped Heal, Cheaper Loop) | **Stop Time**: The world stops for everyone but you: every enemy loses three turns, and you act again at once. | Fate’s Shuttle: +12% magic attack and +10% cooldown reduction. |

| Chronomancer | **Entropist**: Decay as a doctrine. | Final Hour (Quickening Rot, Cold Hour) | **Heat Death**: Every enemy ages out of the fight: 260% MAG arcane damage, Weaken and Curse on all. | Archivist of Endings: +15% magic attack and 8% lifesteal. |

| Artificer | **Runesmith**: Etched metal that obeys. Mostly. | Runic Detonation (Loud Runes, Binding Runes) | **Rune Storm**: The air fills with glyphs: 280% MAG arcane damage to every enemy, the party takes 25% less damage for 3 turns and you shed all Heat. | Runelord: +15% magic attack and 10% less damage taken. |

You steal that it happened. | Mirror Theft (Deeper Mirror, Cheaper Mirror) | **The Perfect Heist**: A job so clean no one notices: 300% ATK to every enemy, steals from each, and you act again at once. | Archthief of Echoes: +15% magic attack and +10% cooldown reduction. |
| Dreamwalker | **Sandman**: Rest is a weapon, too. | Dust of Dreams (More Dust, Cheap Dust) | **Eternal Night**: Night falls on the whole field: every enemy sleeps for 3 turns, and you and the party gain +30% damage and 25% less damage taken for 3 turns. | The Sandman: +15% magic attack and sleep lasts a turn longer. |
| Dreamwalker | **Nightmare Lord**: Make the dream bite back. | Living Nightmare (Hungry Hound, Cold Hound) | **Night Terror**: Every enemy’s worst dream: 260% MAG shadow damage, Nightmare and Fear on all; two Dream Hounds join you. | Nightmare Sovereign: +15% magic attack and hounds last twice as long. |
| Dragonsoul | **Wyrmblood**: Pure fury, four fires. | Draconic Fury (More Claws, Savage Fury) | **The Ascendant Dragon**: You are every drake: all four breaths in one: 300% ATK to every enemy, Burn, Freeze chance and Curse; you gain +30% damage for 3 turns. | The Wyrm Reborn: +15% physical attack and +10% crit chance. |
| Dragonsoul | **Scalebound**: The hoard guards itself. | Adamant Scales (Thicker Scales, Quick Scales) | **The Drake Throne**: You sit the throne of all four: 240% ATK to every enemy, the party is healed for 30% and gains Divine Protection. | Hoard-King: +15% max HP and 12% less damage taken. |
| Puppeteer | **Master Marionettist**: A whole cast, always. | Puppet Army (Third Puppet, Quick Rehearsal) | **The Final Act**: The stage fills: 260% MAG damage to every enemy, a Wooden Knight and a Jester Doll appear, and every enemy is Strung. | The Whole Troupe: +15% magic attack and puppets last twice as long. |
| Puppeteer | **Stringbinder**: Strings on everything, all at once. | Tangle (Wide Tangle, Snarl) | **The Grand Puppet Show**: Every enemy dances: 240% MAG to all, Charmed for a turn and Strung; you act again at once. | The Hand Behind All Hands: +15% magic attack and +10% status chance. |
| Hollow King | **Usurper**: Take the crown from whoever wears it. | Regicide (Deeper Cut, Cheaper Cut) | **Dethrone**: No more crowns: 350% ATK void damage to every enemy, Weakened and Silenced. | The Throne Is Mine: +15% physical attack and +10% crit chance. |
| Hollow King | **Sovereign**: Rule so well that no one remembers the king was hollow. | Royal Command (Louder Command, Quick Command) | **Age of the Hollow**: The hollow age begins: 240% ATK void damage to every enemy, the party is healed for 25%, Hasted and gains +30% damage for 3 turns. | The Hollow Made Whole: +15% max HP, 12% less damage taken and +15% blessing power. |
<!-- specializations:end -->

## General skills (81)

<!-- general:start -->
179 general skills (ranks 1-10; Grimoires and Divine skills 1-5). Learn or rank up by reading a skillbook (consumed) or studying it at the Library (gold + days, kept). Slotted skills train: battle skills by use, passives by 1 per victory (6 x rank to the next rank). Slots: 2, +1 at levels 10/20/30/40/50.

## Multiclassing (8 trials, 13 hybrids)

<!-- multiclass:start -->
Second class at level 15 (third at 40 in NG+2+), after beating that class's mentor in a trial. 45 hybrid prestige classes unlock when both classes reach 25 and the hybrid trial is won. Each hybrid tree is one 16-node branch (2 skills with 2 modifiers each, 4 minors, 2 notables, a choice pair, a keystone and a capstone) gated at 3 points per tier.

### Class mentors and trials

| Class | Mentor | Trial | Mentor skills |
|---|---|---|---|
| Warrior | **Sword-Master Bram**: Forty years a soldier, still never late for drill. | Lv 15: Sword-Master Bram, Bandit Thug | Master’s Riposte, Shield Bash, War Cry |
| Mage | **Archmage Velis**: Tests students by setting them on fire. Gently. | Lv 15: Archmage Velis, Will-o'-Wisp | Lesson in Fire, Dazzle, Goblin Hex |
| Rogue | **Nell the Knife**: You will not see the lesson coming. That is the lesson. | Lv 15: Nell the Knife, Goblin Cutthroat | Now You See Me, Dirty Stab |
| Cleric | **Mother Aurelie**: Kind, patient, and absolutely merciless with a mace. | Lv 15: Mother Aurelie, Skeleton Soldier | Final Judgement, Crude Mending |
| Ranger | **Warden Sylas**: Has tracked the Whisperwood wolves for twenty winters. | Lv 15: Warden Sylas, Dire Wolf | Hail of Arrows, Pinning Shot |
| Necromancer | **The Pale Tutor**: Nobody remembers their name. They prefer it that way. | Lv 15: The Pale Tutor, Skeleton Soldier | Grasp of the Grave, Dark Pact, Grave Chill |
| Monk | **Master Ren**: Has not lost a sparring match in thirty years, or spoken in ten. | Lv 15: Master Ren | Hundred Fists, Master’s Riposte |
| Bard | **Lyra Silvertongue**: Every insult she sings is technically a compliment. | Lv 15: Lyra Silvertongue, Bandit Thug | Discordant Note, Pack Howl |
| Knight | **Ser Haldane Vael**: Has never once lost a position he was told to hold. | Lv 15: Ser Haldane Vael, Bandit Thug | Shield Bash, War Cry, Master’s Riposte |
| Druid | **Elder Maeve Thornwood**: Has been a bear, a wolf and a hedge. Will not say which she prefers. | Lv 15: Elder Maeve Thornwood, Wyld Boar | Root Lash, Savage Bite, Antler Gore |
| Warlock | **Magister Corvane Ash**: Keeps three contracts in a drawer. Reads them aloud when nervous. | Lv 15: Magister Corvane Ash, Will-o'-Wisp | Dark Pact, Shadow Bolt, Goblin Hex |
| Summoner | **Calling-Mother Isaure**: Always has someone standing just behind her. You are not always sure who. | Lv 15: Calling-Mother Isaure, Will-o'-Wisp | Grasp of the Grave, Dark Pact, Crude Mending |
| Alchemist | **Apothecary Dunmore Pell**: Missing two eyebrows and one opinion of safety. | Lv 15: Apothecary Dunmore Pell, Goblin Cutthroat | Lesson in Fire, Crude Mending, Goblin Hex |
| Shaman | **Voice-of-Stone Tamsin**: Hears three conversations at once, and is quietly polite about all of them. | Lv 15: Voice-of-Stone Tamsin, Bandit Thug | Thunderstorm, Crude Mending, Root Lash |
| Chronomancer | **Hourkeeper Vessa Lund**: Is always about to be late, and somehow never is. | Lv 15: Hourkeeper Vessa Lund, Will-o'-Wisp | Lesson in Fire, Dazzle, Goblin Hex |
| Artificer | **Foreman Iseult Brant**: Has one and a half arms, and an unfinished sentence for every machine in the yard. | Lv 15: Foreman Iseult Brant, Bandit Archer | Hail of Arrows, Pinning Shot, Shield Bash |
| Dancer | **Prima Odalys Ferne**: Has not missed a step in thirty years. Several people have missed theirs. | Lv 15: Prima Odalys Ferne, Bandit Thug | Now You See Me, Dirty Stab |
| Inkweaver | **Scribe-Mother Hallowe**: Fingers permanently black to the second knuckle. Insists it is a choice. | Lv 15: Scribe-Mother Hallowe, Will-o'-Wisp | Dazzle, Goblin Hex, Shadow Bolt |
| Witch Hunter | **Inquisitor Sabine Crowe**: Has burned a great many books and apologizes to none. | Lv 15: Inquisitor Sabine Crowe, Dark Cultist | Master’s Riposte, Dirty Stab, War Cry |
| Geomancer | **Stonewife Orsa Keld**: Asks the ground’s permission first. The ground has never once said no. | Lv 15: Stonewife Orsa Keld, Will-o'-Wisp | Tremor, Harden, Root Lash |

### Hybrid prestige classes

| Hybrid | Classes | Skills (modifiers) | Notables | Keystone | Capstone |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| **Spellblade**: Steel and sorcery in one edge. | Warrior + Mage | **Arcane Edge** (Flame Edge, Mana Rend); **Spell Ward** (Riposte Ward, Mirror Ward) | Blade Weaving, Spell Strike | Keystone: +25% damage, -30% max MP. | Spellblade Master |
| **Warpriest**: Prayer on the lips, a mace in the fist. | Warrior + Cleric | **Divine Smite** (Thunderous Smite, Smite the Wicked); **War Hymn** (Hymn of Wrath, Hymn of Mending) | Martyr’s Resolve, Crusade | Keystone: +25% damage, -50 healing power. | Avatar of War: Start every battle with +25% damage for 3 turns. |
| **Deathknight**: A fallen champion who commands the grave. | Warrior + Necromancer | **Death Strike** (Blood Strike, Frost Strike); **Raise Ghoul** (Bone Legion, Death Pact) | Army of the Dead, Unholy Aura | Keystone: take 15% less damage, but healing on you is halved. | Lord of Death: Kills restore 10 MP. |
| **Beastlord**: Leads a pack of spirit beasts into the fray. | Warrior + Ranger | **Savage Charge** (Gore, Stampeding Charge); **Call the Beast** (Alpha Bond, Pack Call) | Pack Leader, Blood Scent | Keystone: +25% damage, -10 accuracy. | King of Beasts: Start every battle with a spirit stag, and the party gains +15% damage for 3 turns. |
| **Templar Ascetic**: The body a temple, the fist a prayer. | Cleric + Monk | **Radiant Palm** (Searing Palm, Palm of Mercy); **Vow of Stillness** (Vow of Iron, Vow of Light) | Inner Sanctum, Zealous Flurry | Keystone: +20% damage and 10% less damage taken, but -40% max MP. | Saint of the Fist |
| **Stormfist**: A monk who caught lightning and never let go. | Monk + Mage | **Thunder Palm** (Chain Palm, Overcharged Palm); **Eye of the Storm** (Static Field, Lightning Reflexes) | Thunderclap, Storm Soul | Keystone: +8 speed, -30% defense. | Heart of Thunder: Each turn, 30% chance to strike a random enemy with lightning (80% MATK). |
| **Shadow Reaper**: An assassin who reaps souls for the grave. | Rogue + Necromancer | **Reaping Strike** (Soul Harvest, Grim Sweep); **Mark of Death** (Lingering Doom, Swift Doom) | Shroud of Night, Harvest | Keystone: +35% damage below 50% HP, -20% max HP. | Harbinger: Your first two kills each battle raise a skeleton warrior. |

## Gods, favor and blessings

<!-- gods:start -->
Favor runs from -100 to 1200. Tiers: Hated (-50 or less), Distrusted, Neutral (0), Acknowledged (100), Favored (250), Devoted (500), Exalted (800); Apostle needs a trial (Phase 9). Gaining favor costs the god's rivals 10% of it. Shrine prayer: +10 once per day.

| God | Rivals / allies | Likes | Dislikes | Offerings | Prayer (battle) | Intervention |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| **Solenne**, Sun & Justice | Morrath / Hesha, Ordrun | Smiting the undead (+3); Ending cultists (+3); Wielding holy light (+0.5); Victories under the sun (+1); Helping people in need (+4) | Raising the dead (-2); Fleeing from evil (-2) | Values arms and armor for her crusaders. | **Sunburst**: Holy light sears every enemy (60% Divine Power). (15 favor) | **The Unclouded Eye**: Solenne’s gaze burns every enemy as she raises you. |
| **Morrath**, Death & Endings | Solenne, Mirrin / Vex, Lyssara | Felling mighty foes (+5); Raising the dead (+2); Shadow arts (+0.5); Victories by night (+1) | Cheating death with healing (-0.25); Holy light (-0.5) | Collects the strange things the dead leave behind. | **Death’s Toll**: Curse and Weaken every enemy for 2 turns. (15 favor) | **Not Yet**: Morrath turns you away from the Last Door, and the shadows take payment from your foes. |
| **Kael**, War & Glory | Hesha, Lyssara / Thalor | Winning battles (+2); Defeating champions (+4); Mighty blows (+0.5) | Cowardice (-5); Defeat (-3) | Loves good weapons above all things. | **Battle Cry**: +25% damage for you for 3 turns, and you gain Haste. (15 favor) | **Not Like This**: Kael refuses you a coward’s death: you rise in a red rage. |
| **Ysolde**, Nature & the Hunt | Thalor / Nymira, Seraphel | Hunting beasts (+2); Nature magic (+0.5); The bow (+0.25) | Wasteful salvaging (-1); Wildfire (-0.25) | Accepts herbs, hides and food with joy. | **Huntress’s Mark**: Mark every enemy Vulnerable and Slow them for 2 turns. (15 favor) | **The Green Hour**: Vines bind your foes as the forest closes your wounds. |
| **Vex**, Trickery & Luck | Grimvald, Ordrun / Morrath, Seraphel | Lucky strikes (+1); Stealing (+2); Uncovering the unknown (+1); Gambling (+3) | Boring caution (-2); Losing (badly) (-1) | Will take anything shiny. Especially gold. | **Loaded Dice**: +30 crit chance and +15 dodge for 3 turns. (15 favor) | **Double or Nothing**: Vex flips a coin for your life. It lands on its edge. |
| **Thalor**, Forge & Craft | Ysolde / Kael, Ordrun | Improving your gear (+4); Crafting (+2); Reclaiming metal (+1); Fire (+0.5) | Neglecting your gear (-3); Cold that cracks steel (-0.25) | Loves rare ores and well-made armor. | **Forge-Blessed**: A shield of 20% max HP and +30% defense for 3 turns. (15 favor) | **Tempered**: Thalor reforges you in the heat of battle. |
| **Nymira**, Seas & Storms | Aurelis / Ysolde | Storm-struck kills (+2); Lightning (+0.5); Frost (+0.5) | Fire (-0.5); Staying ashore (-1) | Pearls, gems and things from the deep. | **Call the Storm**: Lightning strikes every enemy (50% Divine Power) and soaks them Wet. (15 favor) | **The Tide Turns**: A wave of cold water lifts you to your feet and drags your foes under. |
| **Aurelis**, Knowledge & Magic | Nymira / Grimvald, Mirrin | Reading skillbooks (+10); Uncovering secrets (+2); Arcane study (+0.5) | Abandoning an experiment (-1); Destroying craftsmanship (-0.5) | Books, scrolls and curiosities. | **Arcane Insight**: Restore 40 MP and reduce your cooldowns by 2. (15 favor) | **Footnote**: Aurelis amends the record: you did not die after all. |
| **Hesha**, Hearth & Mercy | Kael / Solenne, Lyssara | Healing (+0.5); Resting and caring for yourself (+3); Healing magic (+0.5); Sparing a beaten foe (+10) | Killing people (-0.5); Disturbing the dead (-1) | Food, potions and gold for the poor. | **Hearth’s Warmth**: Heal 30% of max HP and cure Poison, Bleed and Burn. (15 favor) | **Mercy**: Hesha holds you close until the pain passes. |
| **Grimvald**, Wealth & Trade | Vex, Seraphel / Aurelis | Earning gold (per 100) (+1); Profitable work (+0.5) | Losing money (-3); Theft (-1) | Gold. Honest, countable gold. | **Tithe**: Shield 25% of max HP and gain +20% gold find for this battle. (15 favor) | **Debt Forgiven**: Grimvald writes off your death as a bad debt. |
| **Lyssara**, Dreams & the Moon | Kael / Morrath, Hesha, Mirrin | Resting well (+2); Victories by moonlight (+1.5); Sparing the sleeping and the beaten (+4); Moonlit cold (+0.5) | Brawling in the glare of noon (-0.5); Waking people to rob them (-3) | Likes small strange things from dreams, and old books. | **Lullaby of the Moon**: Every enemy has a 60% chance to fall asleep for 2 turns (a hit wakes them). (15 favor) | **Dreamed Away**: Lyssara folds you into a dream; you wake whole, and the fight is slow to follow. |
| **Ordrun**, Earth & Endurance | Vex / Thalor, Solenne | Winning, patiently (+1.5); Outlasting great foes (+3); Building something that will stand (+2); Finishing what you promised (+3) | Giving up the ground (-4); Trusting luck over work (-2) | Respects armor, stone and ore. | **Stone Stance**: Shield yourself for 30% of max HP and take 25% less damage for 3 turns. (15 favor) | **Enduring**: Ordrun sets his hand on your shoulder, and the ground itself holds you up. |
| **Seraphel**, Wind & Freedom | Grimvald / Ysolde, Vex | Setting the beaten free (+4); Knowing when to leave (+1); Lightning strikes (+0.5); Taking a chance (+1.5) | Binding creatures to your will (-1.5); Locking things into place (-1) | Likes things that travel: feathers, maps, letters. | **Free as the Wind**: Gain Haste for 2 turns, and the party gains +20 dodge for 3 turns. (15 favor) | **Carried Off**: A gust lifts you clear; you land, breathless, with the wind at your back. |
| **Mirrin**, Memory & Time | Morrath / Aurelis, Lyssara | Learning what was written (+3); Uncovering what things were (+3); Keeping an old promise (+2); Letting time pass properly (+1) | Letting the story end early (-2); Breaking things with a past (-2); Gambling with what is already settled (-2) | Treasures books, relics and anything with a date on it. | **A Moment Borrowed**: Take an extra turn immediately and Regenerate for 2 turns. (15 favor) | **It Has Not Happened Yet**: Mirrin turns the hour back; the blow never lands, and you stand where you stood a breath ago. |

### Blessings (180)

Minor blessings last 10 battles and cost favor. Major blessings are offered 3 at a time when a tier is first reached, stay active while the tier holds, and fill 1-3 slots (more at levels 25 and 50). Rival gods' blessings cannot be slotted together.

## Apostleship

<!-- apostles:start -->
Exalted favor plus the god's three-stage Trial of Apostleship (two rounds of heralds, then the god's Aspect, fought at max(30, your level) + 0/1/3) make you its Apostle. One god at a time. Apostles get a 20-node tree (10 points + 1 per level after ascension), a Divine Avatar (an ultimate, once per battle), a god-bound weapon that evolves at levels 30/45/60, a Divine blessing in an extra slot, a title and Divine Edicts. Apostasy makes the god Hated and its heralds hunt you for 15 battles.

| God | Divine Avatar | Divine weapon (stages) | Divine blessing | Tree skills | Keystone / capstone |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| **Solenne** | **Avatar of the Sun**: Become the Unclouded Eye for 3 turns: +40% damage, +30% defense, and a blinding flare (200% Divine Power holy) on every enemy. | Dawnbreaker → Dawnbreaker, Risen → Dawnbreaker, Zenith → Dawnbreaker, the Unclouded (Mace) | **Sunsworn**: Your hits have a 25% chance to call down a pillar of light (80% Divine Power). | **Radiant Verdict**: Pass judgement: 170% Divine Power holy damage.<br>**Sanctified Ground**: Holy ground: the party Regenerates and gains +20% holy damage for 3 turns. | Keystone: +30% holy damage, but -20% max MP. / Light Everlasting |
| **Morrath** | **Avatar of the Last Door**: Become Death’s keeper for 3 turns: +40% damage and 20% lifesteal; every enemy takes 180% Divine Power shadow damage and is Cursed. | Last Light → Last Light, Unlit → Last Light, Eclipse → Last Light, the Final Door (Scythe) | **Deathless**: Kills restore 15% of max HP and summon a skeleton (twice per battle). | **Reap the Fallen**: 160% Divine Power shadow damage; heal 10% of max HP.<br>**Raven Host**: Raise two skeletons and Fear every enemy for 1 turn. | Keystone: +35% shadow damage, -15% max HP. / Keeper of the Door: Kills Fear every enemy for 1 turn. |

Heavy stagger.<br>**Warcry of Kael**: The party gains +25% damage and Haste for 2 turns. | Keystone: +30% damage, -10% defense. / Banner of Kael: Start every battle with Haste and +20% damage for 3 turns. |

| **Lyssara** | **Avatar of the Moon**: Become the Moon Between Dreams for 3 turns: +40% damage and +30 dodge; every enemy takes 180% Divine Power ice damage and may fall asleep. | Crescent Rod → Crescent Rod, Waxing → Crescent Rod, Full → Crescent Rod, the Moon Between Dreams (Staff) | **Moonborn**: Enemies start battle drowsy: 25% chance to Slow each. +20% ice damage and 10% less damage taken. | **Moonbeam**: A pale beam for 170% Divine Power ice damage; 30% chance to put the target to sleep.<br>**Moon Veil**: Shield yourself for 25% of max HP and slip into Stealth for 2 turns. | Keystone: +20% damage at night is permanent, but -10% max HP. / Moon Between Dreams: Start every battle with a shield of 20% max HP. |
| **Ordrun** | **Avatar of the Mountain**: Become the Unmoved for 3 turns: +35% damage and +50% defense; every enemy takes 170% Divine Power damage and is Slowed. | Keystone → Keystone, Set → Keystone, Bearing → Keystone, the Unmoved (Warhammer) | **Mountainborn**: Take 14% less damage; 20% chance to Stun on hit. | **Landslide**: Stone falls on the front row: 120% ATK earth damage. Heavy stagger.<br>**Hold the Line**: Shield yourself for 30% of max HP and gain +30% defense for 3 turns; enemies must target you. | Keystone: take 15% less damage, -8 speed. / The Mountain Remains: Start every battle with a shield of 25% max HP. |
| **Seraphel** | **Avatar of the Open Road**: Become the Wind for 3 turns: +35% damage and Haste; every enemy takes 190% Divine Power lightning damage. | Windpiercer → Windpiercer, Fledged → Windpiercer, Soaring → Windpiercer, the Open Road (Bow) | **Windborn**: Each turn, 25% chance to strike a random enemy with lightning (70% Divine Power). +20% lightning damage and +15 dodge. | **Cutting Wind**: A razor gust: 110% ATK + 50% Divine Power lightning damage to every enemy.<br>**Slipstream**: Ride the wind: Haste for 3 turns and +25 dodge for 2. | Keystone: +15 dodge and +10% speed, -10% max HP. / The Road Goes On: Start every battle with Haste for 2 turns. |
| **Mirrin** | **Avatar of the Hour**: Become the Keeper of What Was for 3 turns: +35% damage and 20% cooldown reduction; every enemy takes 170% Divine Power arcane damage and is Slowed. You act again at once. | Hourglass Staff → Hourglass Staff, Running → Hourglass Staff, Turned → Hourglass Staff, the Keeper (Staff) | **Hourborn**: Spells may echo; +20% arcane damage and 15% cooldown reduction. | **Hour Strike**: A blow that lands a moment before it is thrown: 160% Divine Power arcane damage; 25% chance to Slow.<br>**Recall the Moment**: Rewind: restore your best health of the last three turns and clear debuffs. | Keystone: +20% cooldown reduction, -10% max HP. / It Has Already Happened: Start every battle with Haste for 2 turns. |

### Divine Edicts (50)

Each like of your god becomes an edict: do it a number of times within 20 battles. Fulfilled: +40 favor and gold. Ignored: -30 favor and apostle passives at 75% until the next edict is fulfilled.

- **Solenne:** Smiting the undead: 4 times; Ending cultists: 4 times; Wielding holy light: 20 times; Victories under the sun: 12 times; Helping people in need: 3 times
- **Morrath:** Felling mighty foes: 2 times; Raising the dead: 6 times; Shadow arts: 20 times; Victories by night: 12 times
- **Kael:** Winning battles: 6 times; Defeating champions: 3 times; Mighty blows: 20 times
- **Ysolde:** Hunting beasts: 6 times; Nature magic: 20 times; The bow: 20 times
- **Vex:** Lucky strikes: 12 times; Stealing: 6 times; Uncovering the unknown: 12 times; Gambling: 4 times
- **Thalor:** Improving your gear: 3 times; Crafting: 6 times; Reclaiming metal: 12 times; Fire: 20 times
- **Nymira:** Storm-struck kills: 6 times; Lightning: 20 times; Frost: 20 times
- **Aurelis:** Reading skillbooks: 1 time; Uncovering secrets: 6 times; Arcane study: 20 times
- **Hesha:** Healing: 20 times; Resting and caring for yourself: 4 times; Healing magic: 20 times
- **Grimvald:** Earning gold (per 100): 12 times; Profitable work: 20 times
- **Lyssara:** Resting well: 6 times; Victories by moonlight: 8 times; Sparing the sleeping and the beaten: 3 times; Moonlit cold: 20 times
- **Ordrun:** Winning, patiently: 8 times; Outlasting great foes: 4 times; Building something that will stand: 6 times; Finishing what you promised: 4 times
- **Seraphel:** Setting the beaten free: 3 times; Knowing when to leave: 12 times; Lightning strikes: 20 times; Taking a chance: 8 times
- **Mirrin:** Learning what was written: 4 times; Uncovering what things were: 4 times; Keeping an old promise: 6 times; Letting time pass properly: 12 times
<!-- apostles:end -->

## The World

<!-- world:start -->
Eight zones along one road, each a generated tile map with a town or not, dungeons, a world boss, god shrines, a waypoint, chests, lore and a hidden alcove behind a cracked wall. Enemy levels follow yours within the zone's range; `threat` adds HP and damage to a zone's rank and file.

| Zone | Levels | Town | Dungeons (boss) | World boss | Shrines | Weather | Threat |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| **The Meadowlands** | 1-8 | Brightwater | The Goblin Warren (Grukk the Goblin King, lv 8) | Bramblehorn | Solenne, Hesha, Mirrin | Clear, Rain, Fog | 0% |
| **The Whisperwood** | 8-16 | Thornwick | Spider Hollow (The Broodmother, lv 14); The Old Crypt (The Bone Lord, lv 16) | Gnarlroot the Elder | Ysolde, Morrath, Lyssara | Clear, Rain, Fog | 0% |
| **The Sunken Caves** | 16-24 | - | The Drowned Grotto (The Tide Hag, lv 23) | The Abyssal Crab | Nymira, Ordrun | Clear, Fog | 110% |
| **The Ashen Desert** | 24-32 | Sandhaven | Tomb of the Sun-King (The Withered Sun-King, lv 31) | The Sandworm Colossus | Kael, Solenne, Seraphel | Clear, Heatwave, Sandstorm | 70% |
| **Frostspire** | 32-40 | Frosthold | The Ice Palace (The Frost Queen, lv 39) | Skaldr the Frost Wyrm | Aurelis, Thalor, Lyssara | Snow, Clear, Fog | 60% |
| **The Stormcoast** | 40-48 | Saltmere | Wreck of the Leviathan (Leviathan Spawn, lv 47); The Smugglers’ Den (Captain Redfang, lv 44) | The Storm Roc | Nymira, Grimvald, Seraphel | Thunderstorm, Rain, Clear | 45% |
| **The Volcanic Rift** | 48-55 | - | Forge of the Fallen (The Magma Titan, lv 54) | The Ember Matriarch | Thalor, Kael, Ordrun | Ashfall, Heatwave, Clear | 5% |
| **The Dark Citadel** | 55-60 | - | The Black Throne (Commander of the Black Throne, lv 60) | The Unwritten Herald | Morrath, Vex, Mirrin | Blood Moon, Fog, Clear | 0% |

### Zone enemies

- **The Meadowlands:** Green Slime, Giant Rat, Meadow Wolf, Goblin Cutthroat, Goblin Shaman, Hollow Scarecrow, Bandit Thug, Bandit Archer
- **The Whisperwood:** Dire Wolf, Whisperwood Spider, Treant Sapling, Will-o'-Wisp, Skeleton Soldier, Restless Ghost, Dark Cultist, Wyld Boar
- **The Sunken Caves:** Cave Bat, Crystal Golem, Drowned Sailor, Cave Serpent, Myconid Shambler
- **The Ashen Desert:** Sand Scorpion, Desert Raider, Restless Mummy, Sand Drake, Dust Elemental
- **Frostspire:** Frost Wolf, Yeti, Ice Elemental, Frost Giant, Ice Witch
- **The Stormcoast:** Corsair, Sea Serpent, Harpy, Siren, Storm Elemental
- **The Volcanic Rift:** Lava Hound, Magma Elemental, Fire Imp, Obsidian Golem, Cultist of Ash
- **The Dark Citadel:** Void Knight, Fallen Paladin, Shadow Fiend, Bone Dragon, Void Wraith

| **Thornwick** | The Whisperwood | Ysolde, Vex, Lyssara | Wardens of the Wild | - |
| **Sandhaven** | The Ashen Desert | Kael, Thalor, Seraphel | The Iron Legion | - |
| **Frosthold** | Frostspire | Aurelis, Morrath, Ordrun | The Mages’ Guild | - |

### House rooms

| Room | Cost per level | Effect |
|---|---|---|

| **Forge** | 1500 / 4000 / 10000 | Repairs all your gear for free when you visit; +5% defense per level. |
| **Alchemy Lab** | 1200 / 3500 / 9000 | Brews potions each day (1/2/3 per level); +5 healing power per level. |
| **Shrine Room** | 2000 / 5000 / 12000 | Pray to your patron at home (counts as a shrine); +5% favor gain per level. |
| **Trophy Room** | 3000 | Displays world boss trophies: +2% damage per different world boss slain. |
| **Vault** | 2500 / 7000 | An extra stash tab, and +5% gold find per level. |

## The Economy

<!-- economy:start -->
Every town has the core shops; stock rolls daily at your level. Shops pay 25% of value. Prices: value x markup, less Haggle (1%/rank), temple favor (5-20%) and faction rank (5% per rank above Member).

| Shop | Markup | Sells | Where |
|---|---|---|---|
| **Blacksmith** | x1 | Weapons, repairs and ore. | Every town |
| **Armorer** | x1 | Armor and jewelry. | Every town |
| **Alchemist** | x1 | Potions, elixirs, bombs, herbs and alchemy recipes. | Every town |
| **General Store** | x1 | Food, scrolls, keys, bags and cooking recipes. | Every town |
| **Magic Shop** | x1.1 | Gems, runes, staves and wands, and enchanting and jewelcrafting recipes. | Every town |
| **Library** | x1.2 | Skillbooks for sale (or study one you own). | Every town |
| **Temple Merchant** | x1.5 | Divine goods for the faithful: each god’s Divine items (at the devotion they ask) and holy wares. Discounts grow with your favor. | Every town |
| **Faction Quartermaster** | x1.2 | Members only: better gear, cheaper the higher your rank. | Towns with a faction hall (members only) |
| **Gambler** | x1 | Buy unidentified gear sight unseen. Usually a dud. Sometimes a legend. | Sandhaven, Saltmere |
| **Black Market** (night) | x2.5 | Rare goods of no fixed origin, after dark, at a price. Stock rotates nightly. Heretics of the Nameless One are shown the back room. | Thornwick, Saltmere |
| **Odd Bartholomew** | x1.3 | Curios, oddities and the occasional skillbook, out of a cart that is larger inside. | Brightwater, Thornwick, Sandhaven, Frosthold, Saltmere |
| **Madame Vesper** | x1.2 | Elixirs, foods and tinctures nobody else stocks. | Brightwater, Thornwick, Sandhaven, Frosthold, Saltmere |
| **Karl One-Tooth** | x1.4 | Arms and armor off the back of a mule. Don’t ask which battle. | Brightwater, Thornwick, Sandhaven, Frosthold, Saltmere |
| **The Silent Cartographer** | x1.3 | Says nothing. Sells recipes, charts and the raw things that go into them. | Brightwater, Thornwick, Sandhaven, Frosthold, Saltmere |
| **The Collector** | x1.5 | Pays three times the going price for curiosities, and will not say why. | Brightwater, Thornwick, Sandhaven, Frosthold, Saltmere |

### Professions

Pick 2 of the 5 crafting professions (levels 1-50, 10 x level XP per level); gathering is open to everyone.

- **Blacksmithing** (crafting): Forge weapons and armor from ore, hide and cloth. Raises item quality more cheaply.
- **Alchemy** (crafting): Brew potions, elixirs and bombs from herbs.
- **Cooking** (crafting): Cook hearty food from fish, herbs and hunted game.
- **Enchanting** (crafting): Better odds and prices on upgrades and affix rerolls; refine essences.
- **Jewelcrafting** (crafting): Cut gems into finer grades, make rings, amulets and earrings, and add sockets cheaply.
- **Tailoring** (crafting): Cure hides and weave cloth into armor, satchels, snares and cloaks that bite back.
- **Mining** (gathering): Work ore veins in the wild. Higher levels yield more.
- **Herbalism** (gathering): Pick herbs from patches in the wild. Higher levels yield more.
- **Fishing** (gathering): Fish at fishing spots by the water. Higher levels yield more.
- **Skinning** (gathering): Skin the beasts you defeat for hides and meat. Higher levels yield more.

### Gear services

- **Upgrade** +1 to +15: success 100% / 100% / 100% / 100% / 100% / 90% / 80% / 70% / 60% / 50% / 40% / 33% / 26% / 20% / 15%; past +10 a failure drops a level. Enchanting adds 0.5% per level.
- **Reroll** one affix, **add a socket** (up to the base maximum), **raise quality** (Damaged > Normal > Superior > Masterwork), **ascend** (NG+: Legendary or Mythic becomes Ascended). Corrupted items cannot be modified.
<!-- economy:end -->

## Quests, story and companions

<!-- story:start -->
555 quests, 143 NPCs, 207 dialogues, 6 companions, 6 endings.

### Main story (8)

| Quest | Lvl | From | Objectives |
|---|---|---|---|
| **The Silent Shrine** | 3 | starts by itself | Speak with Brother Aldric in Brightwater (talk); Examine the silent shrine (talk); Drive off the Unsworn zealots (fight); Confront Deacon Mott (talk); Defeat Deacon Mott (fight); Report to Brother Aldric (talk) |
| **Whispers in the Wood** | 11 | starts by itself | Speak with Warden-Captain Ysra in Thornwick (talk); Listen to the Heartwood (talk); Put down hollowed beasts in the Whisperwood (kill); Defeat Varn the Hollow (fight); Decide Varn’s fate (talk); Report to Warden-Captain Ysra (talk) |
| **Beneath the Hills** | 19 | starts by itself | Enter the Sunken Caves (visit); Light the rune stones of the Drowned Grotto (runes); Defeat the Shard-Warden (fight); Decide what to do with the shard (talk) |
| **Blades for Hire** | 27 | starts by itself | Speak with Marshal Dara in Sandhaven (talk); Raid the camp (fight); Bring the Unsworn orders to Marshal Dara (deliver); Defeat Captain Idris Kaur (fight); Decide Idris Kaur’s fate (talk); Report to Marshal Dara (talk) |
| **The Frozen Oath** | 35 | starts by itself | Speak with Archmage Oriel in Frosthold (talk); Answer the tomb door (talk); Lay the Wight of Saint Orren to rest (fight); Speak with Saint Orren (talk); Report to Archmage Oriel (talk) |
| **The Black Tide** | 43 | starts by itself | Speak with Harbormaster Quill in Saltmere (talk); Repel the Black Tide raiders (fight); Defeat Admiral Vey (fight); Report to Harbormaster Quill (talk) · timed |
| **The Forge of Severance** | 51 | starts by itself | Reach the edge of the Rift (visit); Free the chained herald (fight); Hear your god (talk); Free the chained herald (fight); Hear your god (talk); Free the chained herald (fight); Hear your god (talk); Free the chained herald (fight); Hear your god (talk); Free the chained herald (fight); Hear your god (talk); Free the chained herald (fight); Hear your god (talk); Free the chained herald (fight); Hear your god (talk); Free the chained herald (fight); Hear your god (talk); Free the chained herald (fight); Hear your god (talk); Free the chained herald (fight); Hear your god (talk); Find the last of the Fallen Smiths (talk); Destroy the Severance Engine (fight) |
| **The Unsworn King** | 58 | starts by itself | Reach the Citadel gates (visit); Defeat the Commander of the Black Throne (clear); Face Seryth (talk); Defeat Seryth the Oathbreaker (fight); Destroy the Shadow of the Unwritten (fight); Stand before the Black Throne (talk) |

### Endings

- **The Godsworn** (title “Godsworn”), with a paragraph for each god’s Apostle: Seryth falls, and with him the last of the Severance. Across the land, shrine after shrine flickers back to life, and the priests swear the sunstones burn brighter than they did before. They tell the story of the Apostle who walked the whole road, from the Meadowlands to the Black Throne, and never once let go of their god’s hand.
- **The Mended Oath** (title “Oathmender”): The Shadow of the Unwritten unravels, and Seryth kneels in the ash where the throne stood, holding Saint Orren’s ring. He does not ask to be forgiven. He goes west instead, alone, to every shrine he and his followers broke, and lights them again by hand, one by one. The gods do not speak to him. He does not need them to. People say that the mercy you showed on the road taught an Oathbreaker how to keep his word.
- **The Unsworn Crown** (title “the Unsworn”): The Shadow is gone, and the Severance is yours: a seed of nothing in your palm. You could plant it. You could cut every thread between the world and its gods with a thought. For now you only hold it, and the gods, for the first time in their long lives, are afraid of a mortal. Seryth sits at your right hand on the Black Throne, and the shrines of the world burn a little dimmer, waiting to learn what you will do.
- **The Free Blade** (title “Free Blade”): Seryth falls, and the Severance with him. No god guided your hand; you did it with steel, stubbornness and friends. The shrines light again, the priests give thanks to the heavens, and you let them. You know who walked the road. The gods know too. Some of them, perhaps, are a little embarrassed.
- **The Twilight Ends** (title “Twilight-Bringer”), with a paragraph for each god’s Apostle: You speak the name. It is short, and old, and it sounds like a door closing gently. The Nameless One stops being nameless, and what has a name can be mourned, and what is mourned can rest. The gods do not thank you; gods rarely do. But the next morning every shrine in the land is burning, and in the margins of the oldest books, where the scraping was, someone has written one small word in a hand nobody recognizes.
- **The Unwritten Crown** (title “the Unwritten”): The Last Light breaks, and ten gods scatter like startled birds. You sit on the Black Throne and pick up a crown that is not there. The Nameless One is beside you, and then it is inside you, and then there is no difference. The shrines stay lit; the priests still pray. But every prayer, now, passes your desk first. Somewhere in the oldest book there is a hole the shape of your name, and nothing shows through it at all.

### Companions

Each companion in a fight gives every enemy 150% more HP (regeneration still heals as if alone) and +12% damage. Companions' attack, magic and divine power, and extra stats, by class (tuned with `npm run sim:party` so every class is worth about the same):

| Class | Power | Extra |
|---|---|---|
| Warrior | -5% | 40% more Max HP, 20% less Damage Taken |
| Mage | +30% | 50% more Max HP |
| Rogue | -40% | - |
| Cleric | -82% | +100% Damage Bonus |
| Ranger | -55% | - |
| Necromancer | +65% | 50% more Max HP |
| Monk | +30% | - |
| Bard | +5% | 60% more Max HP, 20% less Damage Taken |
| Knight | -30% | - |
| Druid | -30% | - |
| Warlock | -30% | - |
| Summoner | -30% | - |
| Alchemist | -30% | - |
| Shaman | -30% | - |
| Chronomancer | -30% | - |
| Artificer | -30% | - |
| Dancer | -30% | - |
| Inkweaver | -30% | - |
| Witch Hunter | -30% | - |
| Geomancer | -30% | - |
| Hemomancer | -30% | - |
| Hollow Hand | -30% | - |
| Dreamwalker | -30% | - |
| Dragonsoul | -30% | - |
| Puppeteer | -30% | - |
| Hollow King | -30% | - |

- **Brother Aldric**, Priest of Solenne: Human Cleric (Saint), follows Solenne, met in Brightwater. Likes smiting the dead, helping people, mercy, healing; dislikes raising the dead, stealing, running from evil.
  - Bond: 20: Steady Hands (+15 Healing Power.) or Sunlit Fury (+12% Holy damage.) · 40: Morning Ward (Every ally starts battle with a shield of 8% of max HP.) or Zeal (+8% max HP and +10 Status Resist.) · 60: Answered Prayer (Once per battle, below 30% HP: heal every ally for 20%.) or Lance of Dawn (+15% Divine Power and +10% Magic Attack.) · 80: Living Saint (+20 Healing Power and +10% Ultimate Charge.) or Unbroken Faith (+15% max HP and +10% defense.) · Oath: Lantern-Bearer (+10% damage and +10 Holy resistance; heals the party 5% at the start of each turn of his.)
- **Fenn Ashwhistle**, Lone Hunter: Beastkin Ranger (Beastmaster), follows Ysolde, met in Thornwick. Likes hunting poachers and bandits, nature magic, sleeping under the stars; dislikes waste, leaving the pack behind.
  - Bond: 20: Hunter’s Eye (+5% crit chance.) or Fleet (+6 dodge.) · 40: Barbed Arrows (+12% Nature damage and +10 Status Chance.) or Pack Tactics (+10% physical attack.) · 60: First Shot (Acts sooner: +12% speed.) or Clean Kill (Kills restore 8% of her HP.) · 80: Apex (+20% crit damage and +5% crit chance.) or Call of the Wild (+10% damage and +8% max HP.) · Oath: Last of the Pack (+12% damage and +8% speed; her crits make the target Vulnerable.)
- **Korga Ironhide**, Sellsword: Orc Warrior (Berserker), follows Kael, met in Sandhaven. Likes winning, beating champions, big hits; dislikes running, sparing enemies.
  - Bond: 20: Iron Hide (+10% defense.) or Heavy Axe (+10% physical attack.) · 40: Come Here (+12% max HP; enemies hit her instead of the back row more often (front row, +defense).) or Bloodlust (+5% lifesteal.) · 60: Never Down (Once per battle, below 30% HP: heal 30% and go Berserk.) or Cleaver (+15% crit damage and +3% crit chance.) · 80: Warlord’s Shout (Starts every battle Hasted, and the whole party with her.) or Titan (+15% max HP and +10% damage.) · Oath: Blood-Sworn (+15% damage and +10% defense; kills grant her a 10% shield.)
- **Ilyra Vael**, Guild Mage: Elf Mage (Chronomancer), follows Aurelis, met in Frosthold. Likes reading skillbooks, identifying artifacts, spellcraft; dislikes destroying enchanted things, cowardice.
  - Bond: 20: Focus (+10% Magic Attack.) or Deep Well (+15% max MP and +3 MP regeneration.) · 40: Frostweave (+15% Ice damage.) or Emberweave (+15% Fire damage.) · 60: Borrowed Time (+8% cooldown reduction.) or Arcane Ward (Starts battle with a shield of 15% max HP.) · 80: Genius (+15% Magic Attack and +10% Ultimate Charge.) or Archmage (+10% damage and +10 Status Chance.) · Oath: Margin Notes (+12% damage and +8% cooldown reduction; her spells restore 3 MP when they crit.)
- **Sable**, Thief: Human Rogue (Swashbuckler), follows Vex, met in Saltmere. Likes stealing, getting paid, gambling, clean work; dislikes getting caught, running without the loot.
  - Bond: 20: Light Fingers (+10% gold find.) or Quick (+8 dodge.) · 40: The Knife Trick (+6% crit chance.) or Lucky Coin (+10% magic find and +5% damage.) · 60: Vanishing Act (Starts every battle in Stealth.) or Poisoned Blades (+12% Poison/Nature damage and +10 Status Chance.) · 80: Ace Up the Sleeve (+25% crit damage.) or Fortune Favors (+10% damage and +8% speed.) · Oath: Partner in Crime (+12% damage, +15% gold find; her crits Blind the target.)
- **Marisol Tidesong**, Singer to the Drowned: Nephilim Bard (Skald), follows Nymira, met in Saltmere. Likes storm magic, music, a quiet night, good deeds; dislikes waking the dead, leaving people behind.
  - Bond: 20: Carrying Voice (+10% Magic Attack.) or Sea Shanty (+8% speed.) · 40: Storm Song (+15% Lightning damage.) or Calm Waters (+12 Healing Power and +10 Status Resist.) · 60: Chorus (Every ally starts battle Regenerating.) or Rising Tide (+15% Ultimate Charge.) · 80: Showstopper (+10% damage and +10% Magic Attack.) or Anchor (+15% max HP and +10 Ice and Lightning resistance.) · Oath: Lullaby of the Deep (+12% damage; starts every battle with a 10% shield on every ally.)

## Bosses, elites, bestiary and achievements

<!-- codex:start -->
### Bosses

Every miniboss, boss and world boss enrages after 40 of its own turns (miniboss), 50 of its own turns (boss), 60 of its own turns (worldBoss), and grows angrier every 4 turns after (Enraged: +40% damage and 10% more speed per stack). Telegraphed attacks take a wind-up turn first: Break the boss to interrupt it, or Defend.

49 scripted bosses:

| The Broodmother | 60%: **The Clutch Hatches** (adds Whisperwood Spider); 30%: **Frenzy** (15% more Speed) | Blight Bloom every 5 turns |
| The Bone Lord | 66%: **Rise** (adds Skeleton Soldier, Skeleton Soldier); 33%: **Death’s Embrace** (+25% Damage Bonus) (heals or shields) | Grave Toll every 4 turns |

Telegraphed attacks:

- **Crushing Blow** (one target, 255% ATK Physical): A colossal blow on one target.
- **Blight Bloom** (whole party, 110% MAG Nature): Poison flowers burst open around the party.
- **Grave Toll** (whole party, 136% MAG Shadow): A bell only the dead should hear.
- **Tidal Wave** (whole party, 144% MAG Ice): A wall of black water.
- **Doom Mark** (one target, 272% MAG Shadow): A killing curse on one target.
- **Glacial Tomb** (whole party, 136% MAG Ice): Ice closes over the whole party.
- **Broadside** (whole party, 153% ATK Physical): Every gun fires at once.
- **Inferno** (whole party, 153% MAG Fire): A firestorm over the whole party.
- **Unmaking** (whole party, 161% MAG Void): Reality comes apart around the party.
- **Earthshatter** (whole party, 144% ATK Physical): Splits the ground under the whole party.
- **Thunderstorm** (whole party, 153% MAG Lightning): Lightning falls on everyone.
- **Piercing Volley** (whole party, 127% ATK Physical): Arrows through the whole party.
- **Shard Storm** (whole party, 144% MAG Arcane): A storm of razor crystal.
- **Final Judgment** (whole party, 160% MAG Holy): Ten gods pass sentence at once.

### Elite affixes

Elites roll 1 affix, champion packs 2 (shared by the pack). Each affix adds 25% XP and prefixes the name.

| Affix | Effect | Never on |
|---|---|---|
| Swift | 30% more speed. | - |
| Brutal | +30% damage. | - |
| Ironclad | +50% defense and +25% magic resistance. | - |
| Hulking | 60% more max HP. | - |
| Vampiric | Heals for 20% of the damage it deals. | - |
| Deadly | +20% crit chance and +50% crit damage. | - |
| Molten | Its hits may Burn; resists fire. | plant |
| Frostbound | Its hits may Slow; resists ice. | - |
| Venomous | Its hits Poison. | construct, elemental |
| Hexing | Its hits may Curse. | - |
| Thorned | Hurts whoever hits it. | - |
| Shielded | Starts the fight behind a shield of 30% of its max HP. | - |
| Regenerating | Heals 4% of its max HP each turn. | construct |
| Volatile | Explodes when it dies, burning the whole party. | - |
| Vengeful | Goes Berserk when badly hurt. | - |
| Unstoppable | Cannot be Stunned, Frozen or Feared. | - |
| Warded | +60% magic resistance and +40 status resistance. | - |
| Necromantic | Raises a bone servant when badly hurt. | construct |

### Bestiary

- Knowledge per enemy at 1 / 5 / 15 / 40 kills (bosses 1 / 1 / 2 / 3): Stats, Resistances, Skills, Mastered.
- Slayer ranks per creature type at 10 / 50 / 150 / 400 / 1000 kills: +2% damage against that type per rank.

(hidden) (Fall in battle for the first time; 5 pts) · Discretion (hidden) (Escape from 10 battles; 5 pts)

**Story & Quests** (13): The Oath Begins (Finish the first chapter of the story; 10 pts) · Halfway Home (Finish the fourth chapter; 20 pts) · The Charge (Finish the seventh chapter; 30 pts) · It Is Written (Reach an ending of the story; 50 pts) · Sworn to the Gods (hidden) (Reach the Godsworn ending; 20 pts) · Mercy (hidden) (Reach the Redeemed ending; 20 pts) · No Gods, No Masters (hidden) (Reach the Unsworn ending; 20 pts) · Free Blade (hidden) (Reach the Free Blade ending; 20 pts) · Helping Hand (Complete 10 quests; 10 pts) · Hero of the Realm (Complete 50 quests; 25 pts; title “Hero of the Realm”) · Odd Jobs (Complete 20 side quests; 15 pts) · Bounty Hunter (Complete 10 bounties; 10 pts; title “Bounty Hunter”) · Loyal (Complete 5 faction quests; 15 pts)

**New Game+ & Twilight** (11): Once More, With Feeling (Begin New Game+; 20 pts) · The Wheel Turns (Reach New Game+3; 30 pts; title “the Returning”) · Endless (Reach New Game+10; 50 pts; title “the Eternal”) · The Silence (Hear the Nameless One for the first time; 15 pts) · Twilight-Bringer (Give the Nameless One back its name; 50 pts) · The Unwritten Crown (hidden) (Take the Nameless One’s crown; 50 pts) · Peace in Heaven (End every old feud of the pantheon; 30 pts; title “the Peacemaker”) · Whispered (hidden) (Become Whispered by the Nameless One; 15 pts) · Unwritten (hidden) (Reach the deepest heresy; 30 pts) · Defender of the Pantheon (Defend a second god beside your own; 40 pts; title “Defender of the Pantheon”) · Every Story Told (Reach all six endings across your playthroughs; 60 pts; title “the Storyteller”)
